# Native control clarification — terminal lifecycle source task

Root ruling: approve a standard-library Unix socket for the genuine same-group survivor case. The controller accepts the survivor's PID message once. The committed control body must leave that peer solely with the survivor after the root exits. After the actual supervisor group SIGKILL, require EOF within the remaining original case/control bound; retain actual native membership, image/getpgid outcomes and EOF observations. This supplies controlled-source termination evidence, not an owning wait/reap certificate for an orphan. Do not claim the controller reaped that child.

No independent watchdog may signal the child into a favorable result. On failure, closing the controller peer so the trusted child's defined read lifecycle exits is allowed failure cleanup. Record that fallback explicitly; its use makes the control unsuccessful/NEEDS_CONTEXT, never compatibility PASS. This is preferable to intentionally leaving an owned test descendant behind. Imports, both original cases, EOF wait and reserved cleanup consume the existing single control/case limits. Any overrun stays a recorded failure with actual elapsed time. No new native attempt, retry or scientific setup restart is authorized.
